{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\ESP32_TURSO_GUIDE.md: # ESP32 ↔ Turso Cloud (Read-Only) Integration & Minimal Rendering Guide\n\nThis guide details how your **ESP32 microcontroller** securely accesses your **Turso Cloud SQLite database** in **read-only mode** 24/7 over Wi-Fi, and outlines the **minimal 5-point metadata specification** for hardware quote rendering.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Security Architecture: Dedicated Read-Only Token\n\nTo protect your cloud database from tampering or hardware theft, the ESP32 should **never hold a full read-write token**. Turso allows creating tokens restricted exclusively to `SELECT` operations.\n\n### Generating a Read-Only Token in Turso:\n1. Open **[https://app.turso.tech](https://app.turso.tech)**.\n2. Navigate to your database (`heraclitus-rien-bluerose`).\n3. Click the **Tokens** tab -> **\"Create Token\"**.\n4. Set Name: `esp32-readonly`.\n5. Toggle **\"Read-only\"**: **ON** (`true`).\n6. Set Expiration: **Never** (or preferred timeframe).\n7. Copy the generated read-only token into your ESP32 sketch.\n\n*(Any attempt by firmware using this token to execute `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, or `DELETE` is automatically rejected by the Turso kernel with HTTP 403).*\n\n---\n\n## 2. Minimal Metadata Rendering Specification\n\nFor microcontrollers with limited display canvas (E-Paper, TFT, OLED) and audio engines, the lesser the metadata, the better. We strip away internal UUIDs, backlinks, and shadow tags, distilling each quote to **5 essential display dimensions**:\n\n| # | Dimension | Field Name | Description | Fallback Rule |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **1** | **Content** | `content` | The core wisdom quote / poem text | Required |\n| **2** | **Context** | `context` | Situational backdrop & reflection | Empty string if absent |\n| **3** | **Origin / Creator** | `container_name` | The author, persona, or franchise (e.g. *William Blake*, *Faust*) | Joined from Table 2 |\n| **4** | **Specific Endpoint** | `specific_name` | The work, chapter, or song (e.g. *Infant Joy*, *Koi no Koe*) | Joined from Table 3 |\n| **5** | **Effective Date** | `effective_date` | The temporal coordinate (e.g. `2026-09-30`, `1789`) | **Display-time fallback**: If quote date is empty, automatically uses `specific.date` without mutating database |\n| **+** | **Thematic Anchor** | `theme_names` | Plain text comma-separated conceptual labels (e.g. *Heartbroken*, *Order & Entropy*) | Resolved from Table 4 |\n\n---\n\n## 3. The Unified Edge SQL Query (One-Trip Resolution)\n\nRather than forcing the ESP32 to make multiple network roundtrips to join tables, we let Turso's SQLite engine perform the join and fallback at the edge. The ESP32 sends a single query and receives the complete 5-point bundle:\n\n### Query: Fetch Random Quote for Rendering\n```sql\nSELECT \n    q.idx,\n    q.main_content AS content,\n    COALESCE(q.context, '') AS context,\n    COALESCE(NULLIF(q.date, ''), s.date, '') AS effective_date,\n    COALESCE(s.name, '') AS specific_name,\n    COALESCE(c.name, '') AS container_name,\n    COALESCE((\n        SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.name, ', ')\n        FROM themes t\n        WHERE t.id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(q.theme_ids))\n    ), '') AS theme_names\nFROM quotes q\nLEFT JOIN specifics s ON q.specific_id = s.id\nLEFT JOIN containers c ON s.container_id = c.id\nORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1;\n```\n\n### Query: Fetch Latest Quote for Rendering\n```sql\nSELECT \n    q.idx,\n    q.main_content AS content,\n    COALESCE(q.context, '') AS context,\n    COALESCE(NULLIF(q.date, ''), s.date, '') AS effective_date,\n    COALESCE(s.name, '') AS specific_name,\n    COALESCE(c.name, '') AS container_name,\n    COALESCE((\n        SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.name, ', ')\n        FROM themes t\n        WHERE t.id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(q.theme_ids))\n    ), '') AS theme_names\nFROM quotes q\nLEFT JOIN specifics s ON q.specific_id = s.id\nLEFT JOIN containers c ON s.container_id = c.id\nORDER BY q.idx DESC LIMIT 1;\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Complete Read-Only Arduino C++ Sketch\n\nInstall via Arduino IDE Library Manager:\n- **`ArduinoJson`** (v6 or v7 by Benoit Blanchon)\n\n```cpp\n#include <WiFi.h>\n#include <WiFiClientSecure.h>\n#include <HTTPClient.h>\n#include <ArduinoJson.h>\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 1. CONFIGURATION\n// ==============================================================================\nconst char* ssid          = \"YOUR_WIFI_SSID\";\nconst char* password      = \"YOUR_WIFI_PASSWORD\";\n\n// Turso Database HTTP Endpoint (Sydney AWS edge)\nconst char* turso_url     = \"https://heraclitus-rien-bluerose.aws-ap-southeast-2.turso.io/v2/pipeline\";\n\n// Paste your dedicated Read-Only token here\nconst char* turso_ro_token = \"YOUR_READ_ONLY_TOKEN\";\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 2. DATA RECEPTACLE (MINIMAL 5-POINT METADATA STRUCT)\n// ==============================================================================\nstruct QuoteRenderPayload {\n  long idx;\n  String content;\n  String context;\n  String effectiveDate;\n  String specificName;\n  String containerName;\n  String themeNames;\n};\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 3. SECURE READ-ONLY TURSO FETCHER\n// ==============================================================================\nbool fetchQuoteFromTurso(QuoteRenderPayload &out, bool randomQuote = true) {\n  if (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) {\n    Serial.println(\"[WIFI] Reconnecting to Wi-Fi...\");\n    WiFi.begin(ssid, password);\n    int retries = 0;\n    while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED && retries < 15) {\n      delay(500);\n      retries++;\n    }\n    if (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) return false;\n  }\n\n  WiFiClientSecure client;\n  client.setInsecure(); // Saves RAM by bypassing full X.509 cert chain validation\n\n  HTTPClient http;\n  http.begin(client, turso_url);\n  http.addHeader(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\");\n  http.addHeader(\"Authorization\", String(\"Bearer \") + turso_ro_token);\n\n  // The 5-point minimal metadata query with date fallback and joined names\n  String sql = \"SELECT \"\n               \"q.idx, \"\n               \"q.main_content AS content, \"\n               \"COALESCE(q.context, '') AS context, \"\n               \"COALESCE(NULLIF(q.date, ''), s.date, '') AS effective_date, \"\n               \"COALESCE(s.name, '') AS specific_name, \"\n               \"COALESCE(c.name, '') AS container_name, \"\n               \"COALESCE((SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.name, ', ') FROM themes t WHERE t.id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(q.theme_ids))), '') AS theme_names \"\n               \"FROM quotes q \"\n               \"LEFT JOIN specifics s ON q.specific_id = s.id \"\n               \"LEFT JOIN containers c ON s.container_id = c.id \";\n\n  sql += randomQuote ? \"ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1;\" : \"ORDER BY q.idx DESC LIMIT 1;\";\n\n  // Escape quotation marks for JSON payload\n  sql.replace(\"\\\"\", \"\\\\\\\"\");\n\n  String payload = \"{\\\"requests\\\":[{\\\"type\\\":\\\"execute\\\",\\\"stmt\\\":{\\\"sql\\\":\\\"\" + sql + \"\\\"}},{\\\"type\\\":\\\"close\\\"}]}\";\n\n  Serial.println(\"[TURSO] Executing Read-Only SQL query...\");\n  int httpCode = http.POST(payload);\n\n  if (httpCode == 200) {\n    String response = http.getString();\n    \n    // Parse response with ArduinoJson (Lightweight ~2 KB buffer)\n    DynamicJsonDocument doc(4096);\n    DeserializationError error = deserializeJson(doc, response);\n\n    if (!error) {\n      JsonArray rows = doc[\"results\"][0][\"response\"][\"result\"][\"rows\"];\n      if (rows.size() > 0) {\n        out.idx           = rows[0][0][\"value\"].as<long>();\n        out.content       = rows[0][1][\"value\"].as<String>();\n        out.context       = rows[0][2][\"value\"].as<String>();\n        out.effectiveDate = rows[0][3][\"value\"].as<String>();\n        out.specificName  = rows[0][4][\"value\"].as<String>();\n        out.containerName = rows[0][5][\"value\"].as<String>();\n        out.themeNames    = rows[0][6][\"value\"].as<String>();\n\n        http.end();\n        return true;\n      } else {\n        Serial.println(\"[TURSO] Database returned 0 rows.\");\n      }\n    } else {\n      Serial.print(\"[JSON] Deserialization error: \");\n      Serial.println(error.c_str());\n    }\n  } else {\n    Serial.printf(\"[HTTP] Error %d: %s\\n\", httpCode, http.getString().c_str());\n  }\n\n  http.end();\n  return false;\n}\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 4. DISPLAY / RENDER FUNCTION\n// ==============================================================================\nvoid renderQuote(const QuoteRenderPayload &q) {\n  Serial.println(\"\\n╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗\");\n  Serial.printf(\"║ QUOTE #%-4ld                                                              ║\\n\", q.idx);\n  Serial.println(\"╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣\");\n  Serial.println(q.content);\n  \n  if (q.context.length() > 0) {\n    Serial.println(\"\\n--- Genesis Context ---\");\n    Serial.println(q.context);\n  }\n\n  Serial.println(\"──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\");\n  Serial.printf(\"📍 Attribution: %s · %s\\n\", q.specificName.c_str(), q.containerName.c_str());\n  \n  if (q.effectiveDate.length() > 0) {\n    Serial.printf(\"📅 Date:        %s\\n\", q.effectiveDate.c_str());\n  }\n  \n  if (q.themeNames.length() > 0) {\n    Serial.printf(\"🏷 Themes:      %s\\n\", q.themeNames.c_str());\n  }\n  Serial.println(\"╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝\\n\");\n\n  // Output to E-Paper display, TFT LCD, or I2S DAC speaker here!\n}\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 5. MAIN ARDUINO ENTRYPOINTS\n// ==============================================================================\nvoid setup() {\n  Serial.begin(115200);\n  delay(1000);\n\n  Serial.printf(\"\\n[BOOT] Connecting to Wi-Fi '%s'...\\n\", ssid);\n  WiFi.begin(ssid, password);\n\n  while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) {\n    delay(500);\n    Serial.print(\".\");\n  }\n\n  Serial.println(\"\\n[WIFI] Connected! IP: \" + WiFi.localIP().toString());\n\n  // Fetch and display a random quote on startup\n  QuoteRenderPayload quote;\n  if (fetchQuoteFromTurso(quote, true)) {\n    renderQuote(quote);\n  }\n}\n\nvoid loop() {\n  // Cycle quote every 5 minutes (or attach to a physical button interrupt)\n  delay(300000);\n\n  QuoteRenderPayload quote;\n  if (fetchQuoteFromTurso(quote, true)) {\n    renderQuote(quote);\n  }\n}\n```\n"}

onclick=\"switchLayout('epaper')\">📟 E-Paper ESP32</button>\n        </div>\n\n        <button class=\"btn\" onclick=\"toggleTheme()\">🌓</button>\n      </div>\n    </header>\n\n    <!-- Metadata Inspect Pill Bar -->\n    <div style=\"display:flex; gap:0.5rem; flex-wrap:wrap; font-size:0.75rem; align-items:center; background:var(--bg-card); padding:0.6rem 1rem; border-radius:var(--radius-sm); border:1px solid var(--border-dim);\">\n      <span style=\"font-weight:700; color:var(--text-dim); text-transform:uppercase;\">Shown Dimensions:</span>\n      <span class=\"chip\">1. Content</span>\n      <span class=\"chip\">2. Context</span>\n      <span class=\"chip\">3. Specific + Container</span>\n      <span class=\"chip\">4. Date (Fallback Enabled)</span>\n      <span class=\"chip\">5. Theme(s)</span>\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- MAIN RENDER STAGE -->\n    <div id=\"render-stage\">\n      <!-- Injected via JavaScript based on active layout -->\n    </div>\n\n    <!-- Raw Payload Drawer (Collapsible) -->\n    <details style=\"background:var(--bg-card); border:1px solid var(--border-dim); border-radius:var(--radius-md); padding:1rem; cursor:pointer;\">\n      <summary style=\"font-size:0.85rem; font-weight:600; color:var(--text-muted); outline:none;\">\n        🔍 Inspect Raw 5-Point Presentation Payload\n      </summary>\n      <pre id=\"raw-payload-json\" style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--accent); margin-top:1rem; overflow-x:auto;\"></pre>\n    </details>\n  </div>\n\n  <script>\n    const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://127.0.0.1:20137';\n    let quotesList = [];\n    let activeQuote = null;\n    let currentLayout = 'editorial';\n    let availableSpecifics = [];\n    let availableThemes = [];\n\n    // 1. Fetch Backend Quotes\n    async function init() {\n      try {\n        const [taxRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/taxonomy`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({})),\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes/list`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => [])\n        ]);\n\n        availableSpecifics = taxRes.specifics || [];\n        availableThemes = taxRes.themes || [];\n        quotesList = qRes || [];\n\n        populateSelect();\n\n        // Default to Quote #6 (or first available)\n        const target = quotesList.find(q => q.idx === 6) || quotesList[0];\n        if (target) loadSelectedQuote(target.id);\n      } catch (err) {\n        document.getElementById('render-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); padding:2rem; text-align:center;\">Failed to connect to local Rose server at ${API_BASE}.</div>`;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function populateSelect() {\n      const select = document.getElementById('quote-select');\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) {\n        select.innerHTML = `<option>No quotes in database</option>`;\n        return;\n      }\n      select.innerHTML = quotesList.map(q => {\n        const snippet = q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 32);\n        return `<option value=\"${q.id}\">#${q.idx ?? q.index} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function loadSelectedQuote(qid) {\n      activeQuote = quotesList.find(q => q.id === qid) || quotesList[0];\n      if (!activeQuote) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('quote-select').value = activeQuote.id;\n      renderCurrentLayout();\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    function switchLayout(layout) {\n      currentLayout = layout;\n      document.querySelectorAll('.layout-tabs .btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`tab-${layout}`).classList.add('active');\n      renderCurrentLayout();\n    }\n\n    // Resolve the 5-point presentation bundle\n    function getPresentationBundle(q) {\n      if (!q) return null;\n\n      // 1. Content\n      const content = q.main_content || \"\";\n\n      // 2. Context\n      const context = q.context || \"\";\n\n      // 3. Specific + Container\n      const specific = availableSpecifics.find(s => s.id === q.specific_id);\n      const specificName = specific ? specific.name : \"—\";\n      const containerName = specific && specific.container ? specific.container.name : (specific ? (specific.container_name || \"—\") : \"—\");\n\n      // 4. Effective Date with Non-Mutating Fallback\n      let effectiveDate = (q.date || \"\").trim();\n      let isFallbackDate = false;\n      if (!effectiveDate && specific && specific.date && specific.date.trim()) {\n        effectiveDate = specific.date.trim();\n        isFallbackDate = true;\n      }\n\n      // 5. Themes\n      const themes = (q.theme_ids || []).map(tid => {\n        const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n        return th ? th.name : tid;\n      });\n\n      return {\n        idx: q.idx ?? q.index,\n        content,\n        context,\n        specificName,\n        containerName,\n        effectiveDate: effectiveDate || \"—\",\n        isFallbackDate,\n        themes: themes.length > 0 ? themes : [\"General Wisdom\"]\n      };\n    }\n\n    function renderCurrentLayout() {\n      const b = getPresentationBundle(activeQuote);\n      if (!b) return;\n\n      // Update Inspector Drawer\n      document.getElementById('raw-payload-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(b, null, 2);\n\n      const stage = document.getElementById('render-stage');\n\n      if (currentLayout === 'editorial') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <article class=\"card-editorial\">\n            <div class=\"quote-header-meta\">\n              <span class=\"chip-accent\">QUOTE #${b.idx}</span>\n              <div style=\"display:flex; gap:0.4rem;\">\n                ${b.themes.map(t => `<span class=\"chip\">🏷 ${t}</span>`).join('')}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"quote-body\">\n              ${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}\n            </div>\n\n            ${b.context ? `\n              <div class=\"context-box\">\n                ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <div class=\"footer-attribution\">\n              <div class=\"source-line\">\n                <span class=\"source-title\">📍 ${b.specificName}</span>\n                <span class=\"source-origin\">by ${b.containerName}</span>\n              </div>\n              <div style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.85rem; color:var(--text-muted);\">\n                📅 ${b.effectiveDate} ${b.isFallbackDate ? '<span class=\"badge-fallback\">(inherited from endpoint)</span>' : ''}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </article>\n        `;\n      } else if (currentLayout === 'zen') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"card-zen\">\n            <div class=\"zen-top\">\n              <span style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-weight:700; color:var(--accent);\">#${b.idx}</span>\n              <span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);\">·</span>\n              <span style=\"font-weight:600; color:var(--text-vivid);\">📍 ${b.specificName}</span>\n              <span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);\">(${b.containerName})</span>\n              <span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);\">·</span>\n              <span style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--text-muted);\">📅 ${b.effectiveDate}</span>\n              ${b.isFallbackDate ? '<span class=\"badge-fallback\">(inherited)</span>' : ''}\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"zen-content\">\n              ${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}\n            </div>\n\n            ${b.context ? `\n              <div class=\"zen-context\">\n                <strong>Context:</strong> ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <div class=\"zen-bottom\">\n              <div style=\"display:flex; gap:0.35rem;\">\n                ${b.themes.map(t => `<span class=\"chip\" style=\"font-size:0.7rem;\">🏷 ${t}</span>`).join('')}\n              </div>\n              <span style=\"font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-dim);\">Prescripts Studio · O(1) Cache</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      } else if (currentLayout === 'epaper') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"epaper-device\">\n            <div class=\"epaper-status-bar\">\n              <span>TURSO CLOUD EDGE · QUOTE #${b.idx}</span>\n              <span>DATE: ${b.effectiveDate}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"epaper-body\">\n              ${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}\n            </div>\n\n            ${b.context ? `\n              <div class=\"epaper-context\">\n                [CONTEXT]: ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <div class=\"epaper-footer\">\n              <span>[SRC]: ${b.specificName} // ${b.containerName}</span>\n              <span>[TAG]: ${b.themes.join(', ')}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleTheme() {\n      const html = document.documentElement;\n      const cur = html.getAttribute('data-theme') || 'dark';\n      const nxt = cur === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n      html.setAttribute('data-theme', nxt);\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"}

q.index} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function loadSelectedQuote(qid) {\n      activeQuote = quotesList.find(q => q.id === qid) || quotesList[0];\n      if (!activeQuote) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('quote-select').value = activeQuote.id;\n      renderCurrentLayout();\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    function switchLayout(layout) {\n      currentLayout = layout;\n      document.querySelectorAll('.layout-tabs .btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`tab-${layout}`).classList.add('active');\n      renderCurrentLayout();\n    }\n\n    // Resolve the 5-point presentation bundle\n    function getPresentationBundle(q) {\n      if (!q) return null;\n\n      // 1. Content\n      const content = q.main_content || \"\";\n\n      // 2. Context\n      const context = q.context || \"\";\n\n      // 3. Specific + Container\n      const specific = availableSpecifics.find(s => s.id === q.specific_id);\n      const specificName = specific ? specific.name : \"—\";\n      const containerName = specific && specific.container ? specific.container.name : (specific ? (specific.container_name || \"—\") : \"—\");\n\n      // 4. Effective Date with Non-Mutating Fallback\n      let effectiveDate = (q.date || \"\").trim();\n      let isFallbackDate = false;\n      if (!effectiveDate && specific && specific.date && specific.date.trim()) {\n        effectiveDate = specific.date.trim();\n        isFallbackDate = true;\n      }\n\n      // 5. Themes\n      const themes = (q.theme_ids || []).map(tid => {\n        const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n        return th ? th.name : tid;\n      });\n\n      return {\n        idx: q.idx ?? q.index,\n        content,\n        context,\n        specificName,\n        containerName,\n        effectiveDate: effectiveDate || \"—\",\n        isFallbackDate,\n        themes: themes.length > 0 ? themes : [\"General Wisdom\"]\n      };\n    }\n\n    function renderCurrentLayout() {\n      const b = getPresentationBundle(activeQuote);\n      if (!b) return;\n\n      // Update Inspector Drawer\n      document.getElementById('raw-payload-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(b, null, 2);\n\n      const stage = document.getElementById('render-stage');\n\n      if (currentLayout === 'editorial') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <article class=\"card-editorial\">\n            <div class=\"quote-header-meta\">\n              <span class=\"chip-accent\">QUOTE #${b.idx}</span>\n              <div style=\"display:flex; gap:0.4rem;\">\n                ${b.themes.map(t => `<span class=\"chip\">🏷 ${t}</span>`).join('')}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"quote-body\">\n              ${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}\n            </div>\n\n            ${b.context ? `\n              <div class=\"context-box\">\n                ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <div class=\"footer-attribution\">\n              <div class=\"source-line\">\n                <span class=\"source-title\">📍 ${b.specificName}</span>\n                <span class=\"source-origin\">by ${b.containerName}</span>\n              </div>\n              <div style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.85rem; color:var(--text-muted);\">\n                📅 ${b.effectiveDate} ${b.isFallbackDate ? '<span class=\"badge-fallback\">(inherited from endpoint)</span>' : ''}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </article>\n        `;\n      } else if (currentLayout === 'zen') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"card-zen\">\n            <div class=\"zen-top\">\n              <span style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-weight:700; color:var(--accent);\">#${b.idx}</span>\n              <span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);\">·</span>\n              <span style=\"font-weight:600; color:var(--text-vivid);\">📍 ${b.specificName}</span>\n              <span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);\">(${b.containerName})</span>\n              <span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);\">·</span>\n              <span style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--text-muted);\">📅 ${b.effectiveDate}</span>\n              ${b.isFallbackDate ? '<span class=\"badge-fallback\">(inherited)</span>' : ''}\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"zen-content\">\n              ${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}\n            </div>\n\n            ${b.context ? `\n              <div class=\"zen-context\">\n                <strong>Context:</strong> ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <div class=\"zen-bottom\">\n              <div style=\"display:flex; gap:0.35rem;\">\n                ${b.themes.map(t => `<span class=\"chip\" style=\"font-size:0.7rem;\">🏷 ${t}</span>`).join('')}\n              </div>\n              <span style=\"font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-dim);\">Prescripts Studio · O(1) Cache</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      } else if (currentLayout === 'epaper') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"epaper-device\">\n            <div class=\"epaper-status-bar\">\n              <span>TURSO CLOUD EDGE · QUOTE #${b.idx}</span>\n              <span>DATE: ${b.effectiveDate}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"epaper-body\">\n              ${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}\n            </div>\n\n            ${b.context ? `\n              <div class=\"epaper-context\">\n                [CONTEXT]: ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <div class=\"epaper-footer\">\n              <span>[SRC]: ${b.specificName} // ${b.containerName}</span>\n              <span>[TAG]: ${b.themes.join(', ')}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleTheme() {\n      const html = document.documentElement;\n      const cur = html.getAttribute('data-theme') || 'dark';\n      const nxt = cur === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n      html.setAttribute('data-theme', nxt);\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>"}

</div>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- MAIN RENDER STAGE (CHASSIS) -->\n    <main>\n      <div class=\"epaper-chassis ep-font-serif font-scale-md\" id=\"epaper-stage\">\n        <!-- Rendered dynamically    <pre id=\"raw-payload-json\" style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--accent); margin-top:1rem; overflow-x:auto;\"></pre>\n    </details>\n  </div>\n\n  <script>\n    const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://127.0.0.1:20137';\n    let quotesList = [];\n    let activeQuote = null;\n    let availableSpecifics = [];\n    let availableThemes = [];\n\n    // State Variables for Live Tweaker\n    let currentSubLayout = 'minimal'; // 'minimal' | 'cardplate' | 'poster'\n    let currentFontFamily = 'serif';   // 'serif' | 'sans' | 'mono'\n    let currentFontScale = 'md';       // 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl'\n    let showContext = true;\n    let isCompact = false;\n    let isInverted = false;\n\n    async function init() {\n      try {\n        const [taxRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/taxonomy`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({})),\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes/list`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => [])\n        ]);\n\n        availableSpecifics = taxRes.specifics || [];\n        availableThemes = taxRes.themes || [];\n        quotesList = qRes || [];\n\n        populateSelect();\n\n        // Default to Quote #6 (or first available)\n        const target = quotesList.find(q => q.idx === 6) || quotesList[0];\n        if (target) loadSelectedQuote(target.id);\n      } catch (err) {\n        document.getElementById('epaper-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:2rem; text-align:center;\">Failed to connect to local server at ${API_BASE}.</div>`;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function populateSelect() {\n      const select = document.getElementById('quote-select');\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) {\n        select.innerHTML = `<option>No quotes in database</option>`;\n        return;\n      }\n      select.innerHTML = quotesList.map(q => {\n        const snippet = q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 34);\n        return `<option value=\"${q.id}\">#${q.idx ?? q.index} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function loadSelectedQuote(qid) {\n      activeQuote = quotesList.find(q => q.id === qid) || quotesList[0];\n      if (!activeQuote) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('quote-select').value = activeQuote.id;\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    // Tweaker Controls\n    function setSubLayout(layout) {\n      currentSubLayout = layout;\n      document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\"type-btn-\"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`type-btn-${layout}`).classList.add('active');\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function setFontFamily(font) {\n      currentFontFamily = font;\n      document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\"font-btn-\"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`font-btn-${font}`).classList.add('active');\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function setFontScale(scale) {\n      currentFontScale = scale;\n      document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\"scale-btn-\"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`scale-btn-${scale}`).classList.add('active');\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function toggleContext() {\n      showContext = !showContext;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-context-btn').classList.toggle('active', showContext);\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function toggleCompactMode() {\n      isCompact = !isCompact;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-compact-btn').classList.toggle('active', isCompact);\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function toggleInvertMode() {\n      isInverted = !isInverted;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-invert-btn').classList.toggle('active', isInverted);\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function updateChassisClasses() {\n      const chassis = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n      chassis.className = `epaper-chassis ep-font-${currentFontFamily} font-scale-${currentFontScale}`;\n      if (isCompact) specificName = specific ? specific.name : \"—\";\n      const containerName = specific && specific.container ? specific.container.name : (specific ? (specific.container_name || \"—\") : \"—\");\n\n      let effectiveDate = (q.date || \"\").trim();\n      let isFallback = false;\n      if (!effectiveDate && specific && specific.date && specific.date.trim()) {\n        effectiveDate = specific.date.trim();\n        isFallback = true;\n      }\n\n      // Prefixed with # for each theme tag\n      const rawThemes = (q.theme_ids || []).map(tid => {\n        const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n        return th ? th.name : tid;\n      });\n      const hashtagThemes = rawThemes.map(t => `#${t}`);\n\n      return {\n        idx: q.idx ?? q.index,\n        content,\n        context,\n        specificName,\n        containerName,\n        effectiveDate: effectiveDate || \"—\",\n        isFallback,\n        hashtagThemes: hashtagThemes.length > 0 ? hashtagThemes : [\"#Wisdom\"]\n      };\n    }\n\n    function renderActiveEpaper() {\n      const b = getBundle(activeQuote);\n      if (!b) return;\n\n      updateChassisClasses();\n      document.getElementById('raw-payload-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(b, null, 2);\n\n      const stage = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n\n      // Format content: Italic and wrapped by quotes \"“... ”\"\n      const formattedContent = `“<em>${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}</em>”`;\n\n      // Attribution: Container on the left of specifics (Container · Specific)\n      const attributionStr = `${b.containerName} · ${b.specificName}`;\n\n      // Tags: all prefixed with #\n      const tagsStr = b.hashtagThemes.join(' ');\n\n      if (currentSubLayout === 'minimal') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"ep-type-minimal\">\n            <!-- 1. Status Bar: Clean #IDX and Date (No \"QUOTE\" or \"TURSO CLOUD EDGE\") -->\n            <div class=\"ep-status-bar\">\n              <span>#${b.idx}</span>\n              <span>${b.effectiveDate}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- 2. Content: Italic, wrapped by “” -->\n            <div class=\"ep-content\">\n              ${formattedContent}\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- 3. Optional Context -->\n            ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n              <div class=\"ep-context\">\n                ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <!-- 4. Footer: Container on LEFT of Specific · Tags with # prefix -->\n            <div class=\"ep-footer\">\n              <span class=\"ep-attribution\">${attributionStr}</span>\n              <span class=\"ep-tags\">${tagsStr}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      } else if (currentSubLayout === 'cardplate') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"ep-type-cardplate\">\n            <div class=\"ep-card-header\">\n              <span>#${b.idx}</span>\n              <span>${b.effectiveDate}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <div class=\"ep-content\">\n              ${formattedContent}\n            </div>\n\n            ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n              <div style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.06); padding:0.5rem 0.75rem; border-left:2px solid currentColor; margin-bottom:1.25rem;\">\n                ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <div class=\"ep-poster-meta\">\n              <span>${attributionStr}</span>\n              <span>${b.effectiveDate} · ${tagsStr}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleTheme() {\n      const html = document.documentElement;\n      const cur = html.getAttribute('data-theme') || 'dark';\n      const nxt = cur === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n      html.setAttribute('data-theme', nxt);\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>"}

</div>\n    </section>\n\n    <!-- MAIN RENDER STAGE (CHASSIS) -->\n    <main>\n      <!-- Initialized directly with the Manager's preferred settings: Classic Minimal + Invert B/W + Mono Font + Size L -->\n      <div class=\"epaper-chassis ep-font-mono font-scale-lg inverted\" id=\"epaper-stage\">\n        <!-- Rendered dynamically via JavaScript -->\n      </div>\n    </main>\n\n    <!-- Raw style=\"font-size:0.85rem; font-weight:600; color:var(--text-muted); outline:none;\">\n        🔍 Inspect Active Quote Data Bundle\n      </summary>\n      <pre id=\"raw-payload-json\" style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; color:var(--accent); margin-top:1rem; overflow-x:auto;\"></pre>\n    </details>\n  </div>\n\n  <script>\n    const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://127.0.0.1:20137';\n    let quotesList = [];\n    let activeQuote = null;\n    let availableSpecifics = [];\n    let availableThemes = [];\n\n    // State Variables for Live Tweaker — DEFAULTED TO MANAGER'S BEST SETTING:\n    let currentSubLayout = 'minimal'; // 'Classic Minimal'\n    let currentFontFamily = 'mono';   // 'Mono' (JetBrains Mono)\n    let currentFontScale = 'lg';       // 'Size L'\n    let showContext = true;           // Context visible\n    let isCompact = false;            // 4.2\" standard\n    let isInverted = true;            // INVERT B/W ON by default!\n\n    async function init() {\n      try {\n        const [taxRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/taxonomy`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({})),\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes/list`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => [])\n        ]);\n\n        availableSpecifics = taxRes.specifics || [];\n        availableThemes = taxRes.themes || [];\n        quotesList = qRes || [];\n\n        populateSelect();\n\n        // Default to Quote #6 (or first available)\n        const target = quotesList.find(q => q.idx === 6) || quotesList[0];\n        if (target) loadSelectedQuote(target.id);\n      } catch (err) {\n        document.getElementById('epaper-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:2rem; text-align:center;\">Failed to connect to local server at ${API_BASE}.</div>`;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function populateSelect() {\n      const select = document.getElementById('quote-select');\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) {\n        select.innerHTML = `<option>No quotes in database</option>`;\n        return;\n      }\n      select.innerHTML = quotesList.map(q => {\n        const snippet = q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 34);\n        return `<option value=\"${q.id}\">#${q.idx ?? q.index} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function loadSelectedQuote(qid) {\n      activeQuote = quotesList.find(q => q.id === qid) || quotesList[0];\n      if (!activeQuote) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('quote-select').value = activeQuote.id;\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    // Tweaker Controls\n    function setSubLayout(layout) {\n      currentSubLayout = layout;\n      document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\"type-btn-\"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`type-btn-${layout}`).classList.add('active');\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function setFontFamily(font) {\n      currentFontFamily = font;\n      document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\"font-btn-\"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`font-btn-${font}`).classList.add('active');\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function setFontScale(scale) {\n      currentFontScale = scale;\n      document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\"scale-btn-\"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`scale-btn-${scale}`).classList.add('active');\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function toggleContext() {\n      showContext = !showContext;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-context-btn').classList.toggle('active', showContext);\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function toggleCompactMode() {\n      isCompact = !isCompact;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-compact-btn').classList.toggle('active', isCompact);\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function toggleInvertMode() {\n      isInverted = !isInverted;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-invert-btn').classList.toggle('active', isInverted);\n      updateChassisClasses();\n    }\n\n    function updateChassisClasses() {\n      const chassis = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n      chassis.className = `epaper-chassis ep-font-${currentFontFamily} font-scale-${currentFontScale}`;\n      if (isCompact) chassis.classList.add('compact');\n      if (isInverted) chassis.classList.add('inverted');\n    }\n\n    // Resolve 5-point minimal metadata bundle\n    function getBundle(q) {\n      if (!q) return null;\n\n      const content = q.main_content || \"\";\n      const context = q.context || \"\";\n\n      const specific = availableSpecifics.find(s => s.id === q.specific_id);\n      const specificName = specific ? specific.name : \"—\";\n      const containerName = specific && specific.container ? specific.container.name : (specific ? (specific.container_name || \"—\") : \"—\");\n\n      let effectiveDate = (q.date || \"\").trim();\n      let isFallback = false;\n      if (!effectiveDate && specific && specific.date && specific.date.trim()) {\n        effectiveDate = specific.date.trim();\n        isFallback = true;\n      }\n\n      // Prefixed with # for each theme tag\n      const rawThemes = (q.theme_ids || []).map(tid => {\n        const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n        return th ? th.name : tid;\n      });\n      const hashtagThemes = rawThemes.map(t => `#${t}`);\n\n      return {\n        idx: q.idx ?? q.index,\n        content,\n        context,\n        specificName,\n        containerName,\n        effectiveDate: effectiveDate || \"—\",\n        isFallback,\n        hashtagThemes: hashtagThemes.length > 0 ? hashtagThemes : [\"#Wisdom\"]\n      };\n    }\n\n    function renderActiveEpaper() {\n      const b = getBundle(activeQuote);\n      if (!b) return;\n\n      updateChassisClasses();\n      document.getElementById('raw-payload-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(b, null, 2);\n\n      const stage = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n\n      // Content: Italic and wrapped by quotes \"“... ”\"\n      const formattedContent = `“<em>${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}</em>”`;\n\n      // Header components: #<idx> ~ <specific> on left, Date: <date> on right\n      const headerTitleStr = `#${b.idx} ~ ${b.specificName}`;\n      const headerDateStr = `Date: ${b.effectiveDate}`;\n\n      // Footer components: Tags on LEFT (prefixed with #), Container on RIGHT\n      const tagsStr = b.hashtagThemes.join(' ');\n      const containerStr = b.containerName;\n\n      if (currentSubLayout === 'minimal') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"ep-type-minimal\">\n            <!-- 1. Header: #<idx> ~ <specific> on left, Date: <date> on right -->\n            <div class=\"ep-status-bar\">\n              <span>${headerTitleStr}</span>\n              <span>${headerDateStr}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- 2. Content: Italic, wrapped by “” -->\n            <div class=\"ep-content\">\n              ${formattedContent}\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- 3. Optional Context -->\n            ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n              <div class=\"ep-context\">\n                ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <!-- 4. Footer: Tags on LEFT with '#' · Container on RIGHT -->\n            <div class=\"ep-footer\">\n              <span class=\"ep-tags\">${tagsStr}</span>\n              <span class=\"ep-attribution\">${containerStr}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      } else if (currentSubLayout === 'cardplate') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"ep-type-cardplate\">\n            <!-- Header: #<idx> ~ <specific> on left, Date: <date> on right -->\n            <div class=\"ep-card-header\">\n              <span>${headerTitleStr}</span>\n              <span>${headerDateStr}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Content: Italic, wrapped by “” -->\n            <div class=\"ep-content\">\n              ${formattedContent}\n            </div>\n\n            ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n              <div style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06); padding:0.6rem 0.8rem; border-left:2px solid currentColor; margin-bottom:1.35rem; line-height:1.5;\">\n                ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <!-- Footer: Tags on LEFT · Container on RIGHT -->\n            <div class=\"ep-card-footer\">\n              <span class=\"ep-tags\">${tagsStr}</span>\n              <span class=\"ep-attribution\">${containerStr}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      } else if (currentSubLayout === 'poster') {\n        stage.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"ep-type-poster\">\n            <div class=\"ep-idx-huge\">#${b.idx}</div>\n\n            <div class=\"ep-status-bar\">\n              <span>${b.specificName}</span>\n              <span>${headerDateStr}</span>\n            </div>\n\n            <!-- Content: Italic, wrapped by “” -->\n            <div class=\"ep-content\">\n              ${formattedContent}\n            </div>\n\n            ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n              <div style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem; background:rgba(255,255,255,0.06); padding:0.6rem 0.8rem; border-left:2px solid currentColor; margin-bottom:1.35rem; line-height:1.5;\">\n                ${b.context}\n              </div>\n            ` : ''}\n\n            <!-- Footer: Tags on LEFT · Container on RIGHT -->\n            <div class=\"ep-poster-meta\">\n              <span class=\"ep-tags\">${tagsStr}</span>\n              <span class=\"ep-attribution\">${containerStr}</span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleTheme() {\n      const html = document.documentElement;\n      const cur = html.getAttribute('data-theme') || 'dark';\n      const nxt = cur === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n      html.setAttribute('data-theme', nxt);\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"}

{"Edit":{"file_path":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\ESP32_TURSO_GUIDE.md","adds":"  // The minimal metadata query with date fallback, joined names, and category\n  String sql = \"SELECT \"\n               \"q.idx, \"\n               \"q.main_content AS content, \"\n               \"COALESCE(q.context, '') AS context, \"\n               \"COALESCE(NULLIF(q.date, ''), s.date, '') AS      \"LEFT JOIN specifics s ON q.specific_id = s.id \"\n               \"LEFT JOIN containers c ON s.container_id = c.id \";\n\n  sql += randomQuote ? \"ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1;\" : \"ORDER BY return true;\n      } else {","removesTruncated":false}}

const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://127.0.0.1:20137';\n    let quotesList = [];\n    let activeQuote = null;\n    let availableSpecifics = = {\n      obsidian: { bg: '#000000', border: '#1e1e1e' },\n      carbon:   { bg: '#07090e', border: '#162032' },\n      charcoal: { bg: '#080705', border: '#262015' }\n    };\n\n    async function init() {\n      try {\n        const [taxRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/taxonomy`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => ({})),\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes/list`).then(r => r.json()).catch(() => [])\n        ]);\n\n        availableSpecifics = taxRes.specifics || [];\n        availableThemes = taxRes.themes || [];\n        quotesList = qRes || [];\n\n        populateSelect();\n\n        // Default to Quote #6 (or first available)\n        const target = quotesList.find(q => q.idx === 6) || quotesList[0];\n        if (target) loadSelectedQuote(target.id);\n      } catch (err) {\n        document.getElementById('epaper-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:2rem; text-align:center;\">Failed to connect to local select.innerHTML = `<option>No quotes in database</option>`;\n        return;\n      }\n      select.innerHTML = quotesList.map(q => {\n        const snippet = q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 34);\n        return `<option value=\"${q.id}\">#${q.idx ?? q.index} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function activeQuote.id;\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // COLOR STUDIO CONTROLS\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function setAccentPreset(key) {\n      currentAccentKey = key;\n      const p = ACCENT_PRESETS[key];\n }\n\n    function setCanvasBase(baseKey) {\n      currentCanvasBase = baseKey;\n      document.querySelectorAll('[id^=\"base-btn-\"]').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(`base-btn-${baseKey}`).classList.add('active');\n      applyColorVariables();\n    }\n\n    function setMarkStyle(style) {\n      currentMarkStyle = style;\n applyColorVariables();\n    }\n\n    function applyColorVariables() {\n      const chassis = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n      const base = CANVAS_BASES[currentCanvasBase] || CANVAS_BASES.obsidian;\n\n      let p = ACCENT_PRESETS[currentAccentKey];\n      if (!p) {\n        // Compute dynamic tag and dim colors for custom hex\n        p = {\n          hex:   }\n\n      chassis.style.setProperty('--ep-bg', base.bg);\n      chassis.style.setProperty('--ep-border', base.border);\n      chassis.style.setProperty('--ep-text', '#ffffff');\n chassis.style.setProperty('--ep-tag-border', p.tagBorder);\n      chassis.style.setProperty('--ep-tag-text', p.tagText);\n\n      // Quote Mark focus setting\n      if (currentMarkStyle === 'accent')    // LAYOUT & TYPOGRAPHY TWEAKER CONTROLS\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function setSubLayout(layout) {\n      currentSubLayout =     <pre id=\"raw-payload-json\" style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.78rem; color:#38bdf8; margin-top:1rem; overflow-x:auto;\"></pre>\n    </details>\n  </div>\n\n  <script>\n    const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://127.0.0.1:20137';\n    let quotesList = [];\n    let activeQuote = null;\n    let availableSpecifics = [];\n    let availableThemes = [];\n\n    // State Variables locked to the Manager's chosen options\n    let showContext = true;\n    let isCompact = false;\n    let reRollSeed = 0;\n\n    // 2-Color Accent Pool for Quote Marks & Container Name: Gold vs Blue\n    const ACCENT_POOL = [\n      { name: \"Sapphire Blue\", hex: \"#38bdf8\" },\n      { name: \"Imperial Gold\", hex: \"#f59e0b\" }\n    ];\n\n    // Vast Independent Color Palette for Tags (Golden-Ratio Spectrum Engine)\n    function generateVastTagStyle(tagName, tagIndex) {\n      let hash = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < tagName.length; i++) {\n        hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + tagName.charCodeAt(i);\n        hash |= 0;\n      }\n\n      const goldenAngle = 137.508;\n      const hue = Math.abs(Math.floor((hash * goldenAngle + tagIndex * 89 + reRollSeed) % 360));\n\n      const textColor   = `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 72%)`;\n      const borderColor = `hsla(${hue}, 70%, 40%, 0.45)`;\n      const bgColor     = `hsla(${hue}, 65%, 8%, 0.9)`;\n      return `background: ${bgColor} !important; border-color: ${borderColor} !important; color: ${textColor} !important;`;\n    }\n\n    async function init() {\n      try {\n        const [taxRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/taxonomy`).then(r => r.json()).catch(err => { console.error('Taxonomy fetch error:', err); return {}; }),\n          fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes/list`).then(r => r.json()).catch(err => { console.error('Quotes fetch error:', err); return []; })\n        ]);\n\n        availableSpecifics = taxRes.specifics || [];\n        availableThemes = taxRes.themes || [];\n        quotesList = qRes || [];\n\n        populateSelect();\n\n        // Default to Quote #6 (or first available)\n        const target = quotesList.find(q => q.idx === 6) || quotesList[0];\n        if (target) {\n          loadSelectedQuote(target.id);\n        } else {\n          document.getElementById('epaper-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:2rem; text-align:center; color:var(--text-muted);\">No quotes found in database.</div>`;\n        }\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error(\"Init Error:\", err);\n        document.getElementById('epaper-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:2rem; text-align:center; color:#f43f5e;\">Client Render Error: ${err.message}</div>`;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function populateSelect() {\n      const select = document.getElementById('quote-select');\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) {\n        select.innerHTML = `<option>No quotes in database</option>`;\n        return;\n      }\n      select.innerHTML = quotesList.map(q => {\n        const snippet = q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 34);\n        return `<option value=\"${q.id}\">#${q.idx ?? q.index} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function loadSelectedQuote(qid) {\n      activeQuote = quotesList.find(q => q.id === qid) || quotesList[0];\n      if (!activeQuote) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('quote-select').value = activeQuote.id;\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    function reRollColors() {\n      reRollSeed = Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000);\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function toggleContext() {\n      showContext = !showContext;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-context-btn').classList.toggle('active', showContext);\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function toggleCompactMode() {\n      isCompact = !isCompact;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-compact-btn').classList.toggle('active', isCompact);\n      const chassis = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n      if (chassis) chassis.classList.toggle('compact', isCompact);\n    }\n\n    // Resolve minimal 5-point metadata bundle\n    function getBundle(q) {\n      if (!q) return null;\n\n      const content = q.main_content || \"\";\n      const context = q.context || \"\";\n\n      const specific = availableSpecifics.find(s => s.id === q.specific_id);\n      const specificName = specific ? specific.name : \"—\";\n      const containerName = specific && specific.container ? specific.container.name : (specific ? (specific.container_name || \"—\") : \"—\");\n\n      let effectiveDate = (q.date || \"\").trim();\n      let isFallback = false;\n      if (!effectiveDate && specific && specific.date && specific.date.trim()) {\n        effectiveDate = specific.date.trim();\n        isFallback = true;\n      }\n\n      const rawThemes = (q.theme_ids || []).map(tid => {\n        const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n        return th ? th.name : tid;\n      });\n      const hashtagThemes = rawThemes.map(t => `#${t}`);\n      const rawCategory = specific ? (specific.category || \"\").trim() : \"\";\n\n      return {\n        idx: q.idx ?? q.index,\n        content,\n        context,\n        category: rawCategory,\n        specificName,\n        containerName,\n        effectiveDate: effectiveDate || \"—\",\n        isFallback,\n        hashtagThemes: hashtagThemes.length > 0 ? hashtagThemes : [\"#Wisdom\"]\n      };\n    }\n\n    function renderActiveEpaper() {\n      const b = getBundle(activeQuote);\n      if (!b) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('raw-payload-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(b, null, 2);\n      const stage = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n\n      // Category-Driven Content Alignment: writing => justify, music/poem => left\n      const catLower = (b.category || \"\").toLowerCase().trim();\n      const isWriting = catLower === \"writing\" || catLower === \"bài viết\" || catLower === \"baiviet\";\n      const alignClass = isWriting ? \"content-justify\" : \"content-left\";\n\n      // Quotation Mark & Container Accent: Random pool of 2 (Gold vs Blue)\n      const quoteSeed = (typeof b.idx === 'number' ? b.idx : (parseInt(String(b.idx).replace(/\\D/g, ''), 10) || 1)) + reRollSeed;\n      const accentIndex = Math.abs(quoteSeed % ACCENT_POOL.length);\n      const activeAccent = ACCENT_POOL[accentIndex];\n      const sharedAccentColor = activeAccent.hex;\n\n      // Update toolbar accent indicator\n      document.getElementById('accent-dot').style.background = sharedAccentColor;\n      document.getElementById('accent-name-label').textContent = activeAccent.name;\n\n      // Content: Italic, wrapped by distinct quotation marks matching the Container's color\n      const formattedContent = `<span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-open\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">“</span><em>${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}</em><span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-close\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">”</span>`;\n\n      // Header components: Ghost (#666) and smaller font size\n      const headerTitleHTML = `<span class=\"ep-header-left\"><strong class=\"ep-idx-num\">#${b.idx}</strong><span class=\"ep-sep\">~</span><span class=\"ep-specific-title\">${b.specificName}</span></span>`;\n      const headerDateHTML = `<span class=\"ep-header-right\"><span class=\"ep-date-label\">Date: </span><span class=\"ep-date-val\">${b.effectiveDate}</span></span>`;\n\n      // Tags: Each tag receives an independent random color from the vast spectrum\n      const tagsBlocks = b.hashtagThemes.map((t, idx) => {\n        const tagStyle = generateVastTagStyle(t, idx);\n        return `<span class=\"ep-tag-block\" style=\"${tagStyle}\">${t}</span>`;\n      }).join('');\n\n      // Container: Shares the exact same color as the quotation marks (Gold or Blue)\n      const containerHTML = `<span class=\"ep-container-name\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">${b.containerName}</span>`;\n\n      stage.innerHTML = `\n        <!-- 1. Header: #<idx> ~ <specific> on left, Date: <date> on right (Ghost gray & smaller) -->\n        <div class=\"ep-status-bar\">\n          <div>${headerTitleHTML}</div>\n          <div>${headerDateHTML}</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- 2. Content: Italic in crisp white, category-driven alignment -->\n        <div class=\"ep-content ${alignClass}\">\n          ${formattedContent}\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- 3. Optional Context -->\n        ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n          <div class=\"ep-context\">\n            ${b.context}\n          </div>\n        ` : ''}\n\n        <!-- 4. Footer: Tags on LEFT (Vast random colors) · Container on RIGHT (Shared accent) -->\n        <div class=\"ep-footer\">\n          <div class=\"ep-tags\">${tagsBlocks}</div>\n          <div class=\"ep-attribution\">${containerHTML}</div>\n        </div>\n      `;\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"}

availableSpecifics = taxRes.specifics || [];\n        availableThemes = taxRes.themes || [];\n        quotesList = qRes || [];\n\n        populateSelect();\n\n        // Default to Quote #6 (or first available)\n        const target = quotesList.find(q => q.idx === 6) || quotesList[0];\n        if (target) {\n          loadSelectedQuote(target.id);\n        } else {\n          document.getElementById('epaper-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:2rem; text-align:center; color:var(--text-muted);\">No quotes found in database.</div>`;\n        }\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error(\"Init Error:\", err);\n        document.getElementById('epaper-stage').innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:2rem; text-align:center; color:#f43f5e;\">Client Render Error: ${err.message}</div>`;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function populateSelect() {\n      const select = document.getElementById('quote-select');\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) {\n        select.innerHTML = `<option>No quotes in database</option>`;\n        return;\n      }\n      select.innerHTML = quotesList.map(q => {\n        const snippet = q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 34);\n        return `<option value=\"${q.id}\">#${q.idx ?? q.index} — \"${snippet}...\"</option>`;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function loadSelectedQuote(qid) {\n      activeQuote = quotesList.find(q => q.id === qid) || quotesList[0];\n      if (!activeQuote) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('quote-select').value = activeQuote.id;\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function pickRandomQuote() {\n      if (quotesList.length === 0) return;\n      const rnd = quotesList[Math.floor(Math.random() * quotesList.length)];\n      loadSelectedQuote(rnd.id);\n    }\n\n    function reRollColors() {\n      reRollSeed = Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000);\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function toggleContext() {\n      showContext = !showContext;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-context-btn').classList.toggle('active', showContext);\n      renderActiveEpaper();\n    }\n\n    function toggleCompactMode() {\n      isCompact = !isCompact;\n      document.getElementById('toggle-compact-btn').classList.toggle('active', isCompact);\n      const chassis = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n      if (chassis) chassis.classList.toggle('compact', isCompact);\n    }\n\n    // Resolve minimal 5-point metadata bundle\n    function getBundle(q) {\n      if (!q) return null;\n\n      const content = q.main_content || \"\";\n      const context = q.context || \"\";\n\n      const specific = availableSpecifics.find(s => s.id === q.specific_id);\n      const specificName = specific ? specific.name : \"—\";\n      const containerName = specific && specific.container ? specific.container.name : (specific ? (specific.container_name || \"—\") : \"—\");\n\n      let effectiveDate = (q.date || \"\").trim();\n      let isFallback = false;\n      if (!effectiveDate && specific && specific.date && specific.date.trim()) {\n        effectiveDate = specific.date.trim();\n        isFallback = true;\n      }\n\n      const rawThemes = (q.theme_ids || []).map(tid => {\n        const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n        return th ? th.name : tid;\n      });\n      const hashtagThemes = rawThemes.map(t => `#${t}`);\n      const rawCategory = specific ? (specific.category || \"\").trim() : \"\";\n\n      return {\n        idx: q.idx ?? q.index,\n        content,\n        context,\n        category: rawCategory,\n        specificName,\n        containerName,\n        effectiveDate: effectiveDate || \"—\",\n        isFallback,\n        hashtagThemes: hashtagThemes.length > 0 ? hashtagThemes : [\"#Wisdom\"]\n      };\n    }\n\n    function renderActiveEpaper() {\n      const b = getBundle(activeQuote);\n      if (!b) return;\n\n      document.getElementById('raw-payload-json').textContent = JSON.stringify(b, null, 2);\n      const stage = document.getElementById('epaper-stage');\n\n      // Category-Driven Content Alignment: writing => justify, music/poem => left\n      const catLower = (b.category || \"\").toLowerCase().trim();\n      const isWriting = catLower === \"writing\" || catLower === \"bài viết\" || catLower === \"baiviet\";\n      const alignClass = isWriting ? \"content-justify\" : \"content-left\";\n\n      // Quotation Mark & Container Accent: Random pool of 2 (Gold vs Blue)\n      const quoteSeed = (typeof b.idx === 'number' ? b.idx : (parseInt(String(b.idx).replace(/\\D/g, ''), 10) || 1)) + reRollSeed;\n      const accentIndex = Math.abs(quoteSeed % ACCENT_POOL.length);\n      const activeAccent = ACCENT_POOL[accentIndex];\n      const sharedAccentColor = activeAccent.hex;\n\n      // Update toolbar accent indicator\n      document.getElementById('accent-dot').style.background = sharedAccentColor;\n      document.getElementById('accent-name-label').textContent = activeAccent.name;\n\n      // Content: Italic, wrapped by distinct quotation marks matching the Container's color\n      const formattedContent = `<span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-open\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">“</span><em>${b.content.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>')}</em><span class=\"ep-qmark ep-qmark-close\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">”</span>`;\n\n      // Header components: Ghost (#666) and smaller font size\n      const headerTitleHTML = `<span class=\"ep-header-left\"><strong class=\"ep-idx-num\">#${b.idx}</strong><span class=\"ep-sep\">~</span><span class=\"ep-specific-title\">${b.specificName}</span></span>`;\n      const headerDateHTML = `<span class=\"ep-header-right\"><span class=\"ep-date-label\">Date: </span><span class=\"ep-date-val\">${b.effectiveDate}</span></span>`;\n\n      // Tags: Each tag receives an independent random color from the vast spectrum\n      const tagsBlocks = b.hashtagThemes.map((t, idx) => {\n        const tagStyle = generateVastTagStyle(t, idx);\n        return `<span class=\"ep-tag-block\" style=\"${tagStyle}\">${t}</span>`;\n      }).join('');\n\n      // Container: Shares the exact same color as the quotation marks (Gold or Blue)\n      const containerHTML = `<span class=\"ep-container-name\" style=\"color:${sharedAccentColor};\">${b.containerName}</span>`;\n\n      stage.innerHTML = `\n        <!-- 1. Header: #<idx> ~ <specific> on left, Date: <date> on right (Ghost gray & smaller) -->\n        <div class=\"ep-status-bar\">\n          <div>${headerTitleHTML}</div>\n          <div>${headerDateHTML}</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- 2. Content: Italic in crisp white, category-driven alignment -->\n        <div class=\"ep-content ${alignClass}\">\n          ${formattedContent}\n        </div>\n\n        <!-- 3. Optional Context -->\n        ${(showContext && b.context) ? `\n          <div class=\"ep-context\">\n            ${b.context}\n          </div>\n        ` : ''}\n\n        <!-- 4. Footer: Tags on LEFT (Vast random colors) · Container on RIGHT (Shared accent) -->\n        <div class=\"ep-footer\">\n          <div class=\"ep-tags\">${tagsBlocks}</div>\n          <div class=\"ep-attribution\">${containerHTML}</div>\n        </div>\n      `;\n    }\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);\n  </script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"}
{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\ESP32_UI_SPEC.md: # ESP32 E-Paper UI Hardware Specification & Layout Engine\n\n> **Target Platform**: ESP32 / ESP32-S3 Microcontrollers  \n> **Target Displays**: E-Paper (Waveshare 2.9\", 4.2\", 7.5\" GxEPD2) & TFT/OLED (ST7789, ILI9341, SSD1306)  \n> **Source Database**: Turso Cloud SQLite (`heraclitus-rien-bluerose`)  \n> **Rendering Paradigm**: Clean Vision Layout (Ghosted Header · Italic Body in Quotes · Multi-Color Tag Capsules · Shared Accent Container)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Visual Geometry & Screen Wireframe\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ [ZONE 1: GHOST HEADER] (Y: 0–30px, Font: 9pt Mono, Color: Ghost Gray)   │\n│ #6 ~ Đàn ông thích mèo, đàn bà yêu chó                  Date: 2021~2023  │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ [ZONE 2: BODY CANVAS] (Y: 35–220px, Font: 14pt Mono Italic, White)      │\n│                                                                          │\n│ “Nỗi sợ lớn nhất của một người đàn ông chính là nước mắt đàn bà. Bản      │\n│  năng của phái mạnh là bao bọc, che chở chứ không phải giẫm đạp, hủy     │\n│  hoại. Bởi thế, đàn ông luôn tránh nói lời chia tay vì không dám đối     │\n│  diện với nước mắt, sợ làm tổn thương người phụ nữ của anh ta...”        │\n│                                                                          │\n│ [ZONE 3: GENESIS CONTEXT] (Optional callout box, Y: 220–250px, Dim Gray) │\n│                                                                          │\n├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\n│ [ZONE 4: FOOTER] (Y: 255–290px, Font: 10pt Mono Bold)                    │\n│ [#Heartbroken] [#Wisdom]                           Spiderum - Linh Vũ    │\n│ (Independent Vast Spectrum Colors)                 (Shared Accent Color) │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Hardware Color Codes (16-Bit RGB565 & Hex)\n\nFor full-color microcontrollers (ST7789 / ILI9341 / LovyanGFX) and monochrome E-Paper:\n\n| Element | Color Name | Hex Code | 16-Bit RGB565 | Role / Psychological Impact |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| **Canvas Background** | Obsidian Noir | `#000000` | `0x0000` | Infinite negative space, zero eye strain |\n| **Quote Body** | Crisp Paper White | `#FFFFFF` | `0xFFFF` | Maximum readability, primary focal point |\n| **Quotation Marks (`“ ”`)** | **Sapphire Blue** OR **Imperial Gold** | `#38bdf8` / `#f59e0b` | `0x3DF7` / `0xFD01` | **Shared Accent**: Random pool of 2 bookending the text |\n| **Container Name** | **Sapphire Blue** OR **Imperial Gold** | `#38bdf8` / `#f59e0b` | `0x3DF7` / `0xFD01` | **Shared Accent**: Harmonizes with quote marks at bottom right |\n| **Header Metadata** | Ghost Slate Gray | `#666666` | `0x632C` | Subdued coordinate row (`#IDX ~ Specific` & `Date`) |\n| **Context Callout** | Muted Ash | `#777777` | `0x7BEF` | Secondary background reflection note |\n| **Borders / Rules** | Graphite Divider | `#222222` | `0x2104` | 1px horizontal demarcation line |\n\n### Vast Independent Tag Color Pool (16-Color RGB565 Array)\n```cpp\nconst uint16_t TAG_RGB565_POOL[] = {\n  0x3DF7, // Sapphire Blue (#38bdf8)\n  0xFD01, // Imperial Gold (#f59e0b)\n  0x05B5, // Cyan (#06b6d4)\n  0x15D0, // Emerald Green (#10b981)\n  0xF9E7, // Crimson Rose (#f43f5e)\n  0xC23F, // Royal Violet (#c084fc)\n  0xFD80, // Tangerine (#fb923c)\n  0xB53F, // Lavender (#a78bfa)\n  0x3FD7, // Aquamarine (#22d3ee)\n  0xF49F, // Orchid (#e879f9)\n  0x8E84, // Spring Mint (#4ade80)\n  0xFDE4, // Topaz Amber (#facc15)\n  0xFA0F, // Magenta (#f43f5e)\n  0x4CD7, // Cobalt (#60a5fa)\n  0xD380, // Ochre (#d97706)\n  0x07FF  // Electric Aqua (#00ffff)\n};\nconst int TAG_RGB565_COUNT = 16;\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Typographic Engine & Category Alignment Rules\n\nThe `specifics.category` column determines the hardware paragraph formatting:\n\n```cpp\nbool isJustified = category.equalsIgnoreCase(\"writing\") || \n                   category.equalsIgnoreCase(\"bài viết\") || \n                   category.equalsIgnoreCase(\"baiviet\");\n```\n\n1. **`writing` (`Bài Viết`, Prose, Essay)**:\n   - Calculate line width and distribute remaining whitespace evenly between words.\n   - Produces a flush left-and-right magazine / book column.\n2. **`music` (Song lyrics)**:\n   - Natural left-alignment. Line breaks (`\\n`) are preserved to maintain rhythmic flow.\n3. **`poem` (Poetry, Verses)**:\n   - Natural left-alignment. Short lines retain their poetic meter and ragged right edge.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Single-Statement Edge SQL Query\n\nThis exact query fetches the entire 5-point bundle in a single network roundtrip to Turso:\n\n```sql\nSELECT \n    q.idx,\n    q.main_content AS content,\n    COALESCE(q.context, '') AS context,\n    COALESCE(NULLIF(q.date, ''), s.date, '') AS effective_date,\n    COALESCE(s.name, '') AS specific_name,\n    COALESCE(c.name, '') AS container_name,\n    COALESCE(s.category, '') AS category,\n    COALESCE((\n        SELECT GROUP_CONCAT(t.name, ', ')\n        FROM themes t\n        WHERE t.id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(q.theme_ids))\n    ), '') AS theme_names\nFROM quotes q\nLEFT JOIN specifics s ON q.specific_id = s.id\nLEFT JOIN containers c ON s.container_id = c.id\nORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1;\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Complete C++ Firmware Implementation (GxEPD2 / LovyanGFX / TFT)\n\n```cpp\n#include <Arduino.h>\n#include <WiFi.h>\n#include <WiFiClientSecure.h>\n#include <HTTPClient.h>\n#include <ArduinoJson.h>\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 1. HARDWARE DATA STRUCT\n// ==============================================================================\nstruct QuotePayload {\n  long idx;\n  String content;\n  String context;\n  String effectiveDate;\n  String specificName;\n  String containerName;\n  String category;\n  String themeNames;\n};\n\n// 2-Color Accent Pool for Quote Marks & Container\nconst uint16_t ACCENT_POOL_RGB565[] = {\n  0x3DF7, // Sapphire Blue (#38bdf8)\n  0xFD01  // Imperial Gold (#f59e0b)\n};\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 2. DISPATCH RENDERER TO TFT / E-PAPER (LOVYANGFX / ADAFRUIT_GFX COMPATIBLE)\n// ==============================================================================\nvoid drawQuoteOnDisplay(const QuotePayload &q) {\n  // Select shared accent color for quote marks & container from pool of 2\n  uint16_t sharedAccent = ACCENT_POOL_RGB565[q.idx % 2];\n\n  // 1. Clear background to Obsidian Noir\n  // display.fillScreen(0x0000);\n\n  // 2. Render Header (Ghost Gray, 9pt): #IDX ~ Specific on left, Date on right\n  // display.setTextColor(0x632C);\n  // display.setCursor(16, 20);\n  // display.printf(\"#%ld ~ %s\", q.idx, q.specificName.c_str());\n  \n  // display.setCursor(display.width() - 120, 20);\n  // display.printf(\"Date: %s\", q.effectiveDate.c_str());\n\n  // 3. Draw Divider Line (Graphite 1px)\n  // display.drawFastHLine(16, 30, display.width() - 32, 0x2104);\n\n  // 4. Render Quotation Marks (Shared Accent: Blue or Gold)\n  // display.setTextColor(sharedAccent);\n  // display.print(\"“\");\n\n  // 5. Render Italic Quote Content (Crisp White: 0xFFFF)\n  // display.setTextColor(0xFFFF);\n  // bool isWriting = q.category.equalsIgnoreCase(\"writing\") || q.category.equalsIgnoreCase(\"bài viết\");\n  // if (isWriting) {\n  //   renderJustifiedText(q.content, 16, 50, display.width() - 32);\n  // } else {\n  //   renderLeftAlignedText(q.content, 16, 50, display.width() - 32);\n  // }\n\n  // display.setTextColor(sharedAccent);\n  // display.print(\"”\");\n\n  // 6. Draw Bottom Divider\n  // display.drawFastHLine(16, display.height() - 40, display.width() - 32, 0x2104);\n\n  // 7. Render Footer:\n  // - Tags on Left (Each with independent color from TAG_RGB565_POOL)\n  // - Container on Right (Shares sharedAccent with quote marks)\n}\n\n// ==============================================================================\n// 3. SERIAL TERMINAL PREVIEW (ANSI HIGH CONTRAST)\n// ==============================================================================\nvoid printQuoteToSerial(const QuotePayload &q) {\n  bool pickGold = ((q.idx % 2) == 0);\n  const char* accentColor = pickGold ? \"\\033[38;5;214m\" : \"\\033[38;5;75m\"; // Gold or Blue\n  const char* ghostColor  = \"\\033[38;5;242m\";\n  const char* resetColor  = \"\\033[0m\";\n  const char* whiteColor  = \"\\033[1;37m\";\n\n  Serial.println(\"\\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\");\n  \n  // Header: Ghosted smaller scale\n  String leftHeader = \"#\" + String(q.idx) + \" ~ \" + q.specificName;\n  String rightHeader = \"Date: \" + q.effectiveDate;\n  Serial.printf(\"│ %s%-44s%s  %s%24s%s │\\n\", \n                ghostColor, leftHeader.c_str(), resetColor, \n                ghostColor, rightHeader.c_str(), resetColor);\n  Serial.println(\"├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\");\n\n  // Body: Italic in quotes\n  Serial.printf(\"│ %s“%s%s%s%s%s”%s\\n\", \n                accentColor, resetColor, whiteColor, q.content.c_str(), resetColor, accentColor, resetColor);\n\n  if (q.context.length() > 0) {\n    Serial.printf(\"│\\n│ %s[%s]%s\\n\", ghostColor, q.context.c_str(), resetColor);\n  }\n\n  Serial.println(\"├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤\");\n\n  // Footer: Tags on Left (Vast random spectrum) · Container on Right (Accent)\n  String tagsFormatted = \"\";\n  if (q.themeNames.length() > 0) {\n    int start = 0;\n    int idx = 0;\n    while (start < q.themeNames.length()) {\n      int comma = q.themeNames.indexOf(',', start);\n      if (comma == -1) comma = q.themeNames.length();\n      String tag = q.themeNames.substring(start, comma);\n      tag.trim();\n      if (tag.length() > 0) {\n        int colorCode = 35 + ((q.idx * 17 + idx * 37) % 180);\n        tagsFormatted += \"\\033[38;5;\" + String(colorCode) + \"m[#\" + tag + \"]\" + resetColor + \" \";\n        idx++;\n      }\n      start = comma + 1;\n    }\n  }\n\n  Serial.printf(\"│ %-48s  %s%24s%s │\\n\", \n                tagsFormatted.c_str(), accentColor, q.containerName.c_str(), resetColor);\n  Serial.println(\"└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\\n\");\n}\n```\n"}

Tags (Golden-Ratio Spectrum Engine)\n    function generateVastTagStyle(tagName, tagIndex) {\n      let hash = 0;\n      for (let i = 0; i < tagName.length; i++) {\n        hash = ((hash << 5) - hash) + tagName.charCodeAt(i);\n        hash |= 0;\n      }\n\n      const goldenAngle = 137.508;\n      const hue = Math.abs(Math.floor((hash * goldenAngle + tagIndex * 89 + reRollSeed) % 360));\n\n      const textColor   = `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 74%)`;\n      const borderColor = `hsla(${hue}, 70%, 40%, 0.45)`;\n      const bgColor     = `hsla(${hue}, 65%, 8%, 0.95)`;\n      return `background: ${bgColor} !important; border-color: ${borderColor} !important; color: ${textColor} !important;`;\n    }\n\n    async function init() {\n      try {\n        const [taxRes, qRes] = await Promise.all([\n
